Full per-model price data read live from each case's **Edit Estimates** page (read-only). Each block = one model+year variant: the parts that case claimed (Item | Part No | RM/unit, franchise) + that estimate's totals. Same model+year deduped; **different year/variant kept separate** (prices differ). Companion guide + method: [[merimen-part-prices]]. Per-unit prices. Covers the parts each case needed (not the whole catalogue), but common parts recur.
